The simulator\n4: runs a drive through `drive()` with the hidden values; a controller can run the same function\n5: with its own estimates, so nobody has to guess how the published formulas are \n14: TERRAIN_CODES = {\"b\": \"bedrock\", \"r\": \"regolith\", \"s\": \"sand\", \"k\": \"rocks\"}\n15: \n16: # Published physics. `rules()` returns this table unchanged.\n17: PHYSICS: Dict[str, Any] = {\n18:     \"segment_m\": 0.25,                  # a drive is integrated in steps of this many wheel metres\n19:     \"max_tilt\": 0.36,                   # rise over run (about 20 degrees); steeper \"slip_grade_gain\": {\"bedrock\": 0.8, \"regolith\": 1.5, \"sand\": 3.5, \"rocks\": 1.2},\n23:     \"slip_noise\": 0.02,                 # one uniform draw in +-this per executed drive\n24:     \"slip_max\": 0.95,\n25:     \"side_slip_gain\": {\"bedrock\": 0.1, \"regolith\": 0.3, \"sand\": 0.8, \"rocks\": 0.2},\n26:     \"drive_cost_per_wheel_m\": {\"bedrock\": 0.30, \"regolith\": 0.36, height grid with bilinear interpolation, and one terrain class per grid cell.\n33: \n34:     `elevation_cm[j][i]` is the height in centimetres at x = i * cell, y = j * cell.\n35:     `terrain[j][i]` is the class code of the cell with that vertex as its south-west corner.\n36:     \"\"\"\n37: \n38:     def __init__(self, cell: float, elevation_cm: Sequence[Sequence[int]], terrain: Sequence[str]) -> None:\n39:         self.cell = float(cell)\n40:         self.h: List[List[float]] = [[value / 100.0 for value in row] for row in elevation_cm]\n41:         self.ny, self.nx = len(self.h), self.codes: List[str] = [str(row) for row in terrain]\n47:         self.width = (self.nx - 1) * self.cell\n48:         self.height = (self.ny - 1) * self.cell\n49: \n50:     def inside(self, x: float, y: float) -> bool:\n51:         return 0.0 <= x <= self.width and 0.0 <= y <= self.height\n52: \n53:     def locate(self, x: float, y: float) -> Tuple[int, int, float, float]:\n54:         \"\"\"The cell (i, j) holding the point and the point's fractions inside it.\"\"\"\n55:         fx, fy = x / self.cell, y / self.cell\n56:         i = min(self.nx - 2, max(0, int(fx)))\n57:         j = min(self.ny - 2, max(0, int(fy)))\n58:         return i, j, fx - i, fy - j\n59: \n60:     def sample(self, x: float, y: float) -> Tuple[float, float, float, str]:\n61:         \"\"\"Height,         height = (h00 * (1 - tx) + h10 * tx) * (1 - ty) + (h01 * (1 - tx) + h11 * tx) * ty\n70:         gx = ((h10 - h00) * (1 - ty) + (h11 - h01) * ty) / cell\n71:         gy = ((h01 - h00) * (1 -     def slope(self, x: float, y: float) -> float:\n78:         _, gx, gy, _ = self.sample(x, y)\n79:         return math.hypot(gx, gy)\n80: \n81:     def terrain(self, x: float, y: float) -> str:\n82:         return TERRAIN_CODES[self.sample(x, y)[3]]\n83: \n84:     def cell_max_slope(self, i: int, j: int) -> float:\n85:         \"\"\"The steepest slope anywhere in cell (i, j). On a bilinear patch that is at a corner.\"\"\"\n86:         low, high, cell = self.h[j], self.h[j + 1], self.cell\n87:         h00, h10, h01, h11 = low[i], low[i + 1], high[i], high[i + 1]\n88:         south, north = (h10 - h00) / cell, (h11 - h01) / cell\n89:         west, east = (h01 - h00) / cell, (h11 - h10) / cell\n90:         return max(math.hypot(south, west), math.hypot(south, east),\n91:                    math.hypot(north, west), math.hypot(north, east))\n92: \n93: \n94: def drive(surface: Surface, physics: Dict[str, Any], x: float, y: float, heading_deg: float, metres: float,\n95:           slip_base: Dict[str, float], noise: float = 0.0, battery: Optional[float] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n96:     \"\"\"One drive command: `metres` of wheel travel at `heading_deg` from (x, y).\n97: \n98:     Per step of `segment_m` wheel metres, with the ground sampled where the step starts:\n99:       grade  = slope along the heading (rise over run), cross = slope to the left of it\n100:       slip   = clip(slip_base[terrain] + slip_grade_gain[terrain] * max(0, grade) + noise, 0, slip_max)\n101:       the rover moves segment * (1 - slip) along the heading and\n102:       segment * side_slip_gain[terrain] * cross to the right (downhill across the heading)\n103:       energy = segment * drive_cost_per_wheel_m[terrain] + climb_cost_per_m * max(0, height gained)\n104:     The step is refused, and the drive ends, if it would leave the map (`stop` = \"map_boundary\")\n105:     or end on ground steeper than `max_tilt` (`stop` = \"tilt\"). With a `battery`, the drive also\n106:     ends when the battery is spent (`stop` = \"battery\").\n107: \n108:     Returns the end position, wheel and ground metres, the energy used and `stop` (None if the\n109:     whole command ran).\n110:     \"\"\"\n111:     radians = math.radians(heading_deg)\n112:     ux, uy = math.cos(radians), math.sin(radians)\n113:     segment_full = physics[\"segment_m\"]\n114:     gain, side, cost = physics[\"slip_grade_gain\"], physics[\"side_slip_gain\"], physics[\"drive_cost_per_wheel_m\"]\n115:     climb, slip_max, max_tilt = physics[\"climb_cost_per_m\"], physics[\"slip_max\"], physics[\"max_tilt\"]\n116:     width, height = surface.width, surface.height\n117:     remaining = float(metres)\n118:     wheel = ground = energy = 0.0\n119:     stop = None\n120:     here, gx, gy, code = surface.sample(x, y)\n121:     while remaining > 1e-9:\n122:         segment = min(segment_full, remaining)\n123:         name = TERRAIN_CODES[code]\n124:         grade = gx * ux + gy * uy\n125:         cross = -gx * uy + gy * ux\n126:         slip = slip_base[name] + gain[name] * (grade if grade > 0 else 0.0) + noise\n127:         slip = 0.0 if slip < 0 else (slip_max if slip > slip_max else slip)\n128:         forward = segment * (1.0 - slip)\n129:         sideways = segment * side[name] * cross\n130:         next_x = x + ux * forward + uy * sideways\n131:         next_y = y + uy * forward - ux * sideways\n132:         if not (0.0 <= next_x <= width and 0.0 <= next_y <= height):\n133:             stop = \"map_boundary\"\n134:             break\n135:         there, next_gx, next_gy, next_code = surface.sample(next_x, next_y)\n136:         if math.hypot(next_gx, next_gy) > max_tilt:\n137:             stop = \"tilt\"\n138:             break\n139:         spent = segment * cost[name] + climb * (there - here if there > here else 0.0)\n140:         if battery is not None and spent >= battery - energy:\n141:             energy = battery\n142:             wheel += segment\n143:             ground += math.hypot(next_x - x, next_y - y)\n144:             x, y = next_x, next_y\n145:             stop = \"battery\"\n146:             break\n147:         energy += spent\n148:         wheel += segment\n149:         ground += math.hypot(next_x - `elevation_cm[j][i]` is the height in centimetres at x = i * cell, y = j * cell.\n    `terrain[j][i]` is the class code of the cell with that vertex as its south-west corner.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, cell: float, elevation_cm: Sequence[Sequence[int]], terrain: Sequence[str]) -> None:\n        self.cell = float(cell)\n        self.h: List[List[float]] = [[value / 100.0 for value in row] for row in elevation_cm]\n        self.ny, self.nx = len(self.h), len(self.h[0])\n        if self.ny < 2 or self.nx < 2 or any(len(row) != self.nx for row in self.h):\n            raise ValueError(\"the elevation grid must be rectangular, at least 2 by 2\")\n        if len(terrain) != self.ny - 1 or any(len(row) != self.nx - 1 for row in terrain):\n            raise * self.cell\n\n    def inside(self, x: float, y: float) -> bool:\n        return 0.0 <= x <= self.width and 0.0 <= y <= self.height\n\n    def locate(self, x: float, y: float) -> Tuple[int, int, float, float]:\n        \"\"\"The cell (i, j) holding the point and the point's fractions inside it.\"\"\"\n        fx, fy = x / self.cell, y / self.cell\n        i = min(self.nx - 2, max(0, d(height)/dx, d(height)/dy and terrain code at a point.\"\"\"\n        cell = self.cell\n        fx, fy = x / cell, y / cell\n        i = min(self.nx - 2, max(0, int(fx)))\n        j = min(self.ny - tx) + h10 * tx) * (1 - ty) + (h01 * (1 - tx) + h11 * tx) * ty\n        gx = ((h10 - h00) * (1 - ty) + (h11 - h01) * ty) / cell\n        gy = ((h01 - h00) * (1 - tx) + (h11 - h10) * tx) / cell\n cell_max_slope(self, i: int, j: int) -> float:\n        \"\"\"The steepest slope anywhere in cell (i, j). On a bilinear patch that is at a corner.\"\"\"\n        low, high, cell = self.h[j], self.h[j + 1], self.cell\n        h00, h10, h01, h11 = low[i], low[i + 1], high[i], high[i + 1]\n        south, north = (h10 - h00) / cell, (h11 - h01) / cell\n        west, east = (h01 - h00) / cell, (h11 - h10) / cell\n        return max(math.hypot(south, west), math.hypot(south, east),\n                   math.hypot(north, west), math.hypot(north, east))\n\n\ndef drive(surface: Surface, physics: Dict[str, Any], x: float, y: float, heading_deg: float, metres: float,\n          slip_base: Dict[str, float], noise: float = 0.0, battery: Optional[float] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"One drive command: `metres` of wheel travel at `heading_deg` from (x, y).\n\n    Per step of `segment_m` wheel metres, with the ground sampled where the step starts:\n      grade  = slope along the heading (rise over run), cross = slope to the left of it\n      slip   = clip(slip_base[terrain] + slip_grade_gain[terrain] * max(0, grade) + noise, 0, slip_max)\n      the rover moves segment * (1 - slip) along the heading and\n      segment * side_slip_gain[terrain] * cross to the right (downhill across the heading)\n      energy = segment * drive_cost_per_wheel_m[terrain] + climb_cost_per_m * max(0, height gained)\n    The step is refused, and the drive ends, if it would leave the map (`stop` = \"map_boundary\")\n    or end on ground steeper than `max_tilt` (`stop` = \"tilt\"). With a `battery`, the drive also\n    ends when the battery is spent (`stop` = \"battery\").\n\n    Returns the end position, wheel and ground metres, the energy used and `stop` (None if the\n    whole command ran).\n    \"\"\"\n    radians = math.radians(heading_deg)\n    ux, uy = math.cos(radians), math.sin(radians)\n    segment_full = physics[\"segment_m\"]\n    gain, side, cost = physics[\"slip_grade_gain\"], physics[\"side_slip_gain\"], physics[\"drive_cost_per_wheel_m\"]\n    climb, slip_max, max_tilt = physics[\"climb_cost_per_m\"], physics[\"slip_max\"], physics[\"max_tilt\"]\n    width, height = surface.width, surface.height\n    remaining = float(metres)\n    wheel = ground = energy = 0.0\n    stop = None\n    here, gx, gy, code = surface.sample(x, y)\n    while remaining > 1e-9:\n        segment = min(segment_full, remaining)\n        name = TERRAIN_CODES[code]\n        grade = gx * ux + gy * uy\n        cross = -gx * uy + gy * ux\n        slip = slip_base[name] + gain[name] * (grade if grade > 0 else 0.0) + noise\n        slip = 0.0 if slip < 0 else (slip_max if slip > slip_max else slip)\n        forward = segment * (1.0 - slip)\n        sideways = segment * side[name] * cross\n        next_x = x + ux * forward + uy * sideways\n        next_y = y + uy * forward - ux * sideways\n        if not (0.0 <= next_x <= width and 0.0 <= next_y <= height):\n            stop = \"map_boundary\"\n            break\n        there, next_gx, next_gy, next_code = surface.sample(next_x, next_y)\n        if math.hypot(next_gx, next_gy) > max_tilt:\n            stop = \"tilt\"\n            break\n        spent = segment * cost[name] + climb * (there - here if there > here else 0.0)\n        if battery is not None and spent >= battery - energy:\n            energy = battery\n            wheel += segment\n            ground += math.hypot(next_x - x, next_y - y)\n            x, y = next_x, next_y\n            stop = \"battery\"\n            break\n        energy += spent\n        wheel += segment\n        ground += math.hypot(next_x - x, next_y - y)\n        remaining -= segment\n        x, y, here, gx, gy, code = next_x, next_y, there, next_gx, next_gy, next_code\n    return {\"x\": x, \"y\": y, \"wheel\": wheel, \"ground\": ground, \"energy\": energy, \"stop\": stop}","lineStart":1,"lineEnd":152,"totalLines":152,"truncated":false}},"title":"app/lightlag_surface.py","time":{"start":1794709646360,"end":1794709646365}},"id":"prt_1dd07ec140013Pj6r3HmmMStlI","sessionID":"ses_e22f8470affe9Z0lsxLmTRHQlm","messageID":"msg_1dd07bb40001sInI4CcxvBwDgw"}}

- y0) * frac\n            c = self.get_clearance(sx, sy)\n            if c < min_clear:\n                return None\n            if s < n_sub:\n                mx = x0 + (x1 - x0) * ((s + 0.5) / n_sub)\n                my = y0 + (y1 - y0) * ((s + 0.5) / n_sub)\n                mc = self.get_clearance(mx, my)\n                if mc < min_clear:\n                    return None\n                _, gx, gy, code = self.surf.sample(mx, my)\n                tname = TERRAIN_CODES[code]\n                grade = gx * ux + gy * uy\n                slip = slip_base[tname] + gain[tname] * max(0.0, grade)\n                slip = min(0.85, max(0.0, slip))\n                wheel_m = sub_d / (1.0 - slip)\n                # Add a slight soft penalty if clearance < 10.0m so routes stay well away from scarps/craters\n                penalty = 1.0 + (0.08 * (10.0 - mc) if mc < 10.0 else 0.0)\n                ticks_cost += (wheel_m / 5.0) * penalty\n        return ticks_cost\n\n    def rebuild_graph(self, slip_base: Dict[str, float]) -> None:\n        self.slip_base = dict(slip_base)\n        self._dijkstra_cache.clear()\n        for j in range(self.ny):\n            y0 = j * self.step\n            for i in range(self.nx):\n                self.adj[j][i].clear()\n                x0 = i * self.step\n                if self.get_clearance(x0, y0) < 6.5:\n                    continue\n                for di, dj in self.moves:\n                    ni, nj = i + di, j + dj\n                    if not (0 <= ni < self.nx and 0 <= nj < self.ny):\n                        continue\n                    x1, y1 = ni * self.step, nj * self.step\n                    tc = self.edge_cost(x0, y0, x1, y1, slip_base, min_clear=6.5)\n                    if tc is not None:\n                        self.adj[j][i].append((ni, nj, tc))\n\n    def nearest_valid_node(self, x: float, y: float) -> Tuple[int, int]:\n        ci = min(self.nx - 1, max(0, int(round(x / self.step))))\n        cj = min(self.ny - 1, max(0, int(round(y / self.step))))\n        if self.adj[cj][ci]:\n            return ci, cj\n        best = (ci, cj)\n        best_d = 1e9\n        for r in range(1, 10):\n            for dj in range(-r, r + 1):\n                for di in range(-r, r + 1):\n                    ni, nj = ci + di, cj + dj\n                    if 0 <= ni < self.nx and 0 <= nj < self.ny and self.adj[nj][ni]:\n                        d = math.hypot(ni * self.step - x, nj * self.step - y)\n                        if d < best_d:\n                            best_d = d\n                            best = (ni, nj)\n            if best_d < 1e9:\n                break\n        return best\n\n    def dijkstra(self, si: int, sj: int):\n        key = (si, sj)\n        if key in self._dijkstra_cache:\n            return self._dijkstra_cache[key]\n        dist_t = [[1e9] * self.nx for _ in range(self.ny)]\n        parent: List[List[Optional[Tuple[int, int]]]] = [[None] * self.nx for _ in range(self.ny)]\n        dist_t[sj][si] = 0.0\n        pq = [(0.0, si, sj)]\n        while pq:\n            d, i, j = heapq.heappop(pq)\n            if d > dist_t[j][i] + 1e-6:\n                continue\n            for ni, nj, tc in self.adj[j][i]:\n                nd = d + tc\n                if nd < dist_t[nj][ni]:\n                    dist_t[nj][ni] = nd\n                    parent[nj][ni] = (i, j)\n                    heapq.heappush(pq, (nd, ni, nj))\n        self._dijkstra_cache[key] = (dist_t, parent)\n        return dist_t, parent\n\n    def travel_ticks(self, x0: float, y0: float, x1: float, y1: float) -> float:\n        si, sj = self.nearest_valid_node(x0, y0)\n        ti, tj = self.nearest_valid_node(x1, y1)\n        dist_t, _ = self.dijkstra(si, sj)\n        return dist_t[tj][ti]\n\n    def find_path(self, x0: float, y0: float, x1: float, y1: float) -> List[Tuple[float, float]]:\n        # Direct line check first\n        direct_c = self.edge_cost(x0, y0, x1, y1, self.slip_base, min_clear=7.5)\n        si, sj = self.nearest_valid_node(x0, y0)\n        ti, tj = self.nearest_valid_node(x1, y1)\n        dist_t, parent = self.dijkstra(si, sj)\n        grid_c = dist_t[tj][ti]\n        if direct_c is not None and direct_c <= grid_c * 1.03 + 0.3:\n            return [(x0, y0), (x1, y1)]\n\n        # Reconstruct grid path\n        pts = [(x1, y1)]\n        cur = (ti, tj)\n        while cur is not None:\n            ci, cj = cur\n            pts.append((ci * self.step, cj * self.step))\n            if cur == (si, sj):\n                break\n            cur = parent[cj][ci]\n        pts.append((x0, y0))\n        pts.reverse()\n\n        # String-pulling / shortcutting where direct edge cost is within 1.5% of path cost and clearance >= 7.5m\n        # Precompute cumulative path cost\n        cum_c = [0.0] * len(pts)\n        for k in range(len(pts) - 1):\n            ec = self.edge_cost(pts[k][0], pts[k][1], pts[k+1][0], pts[k+1][1], self.slip_base, min_clear=5.0)\n            cum_c[k+1] = cum_c[k] + (ec if ec is not None else 1.0)\n\n        smoothed = [pts[0]]\n        idx = 0\n        while idx < len(pts) - 1:\n            best_next = idx + 1\n            for nxt in range(len(pts) - 1, idx + 1, -1):\n                if math.hypot(pts[nxt][0] - pts[idx][0], pts[nxt][1] - pts[idx][1]) > 45.0:\n                    continue\n                dc = self.edge_cost(pts[idx][0], pts[idx][1], pts[nxt][0], pts[nxt][1], self.slip_base, min_clear=7.5)\n                if dc is not None and dc <= (cum_c[nxt] - cum_c[idx]) * 1.015 + 0.05:\n                    best_next = nxt\n                    break\n            smoothed.append(pts[best_next])\n            idx = best_next\n        return smoothed\n\n\nclass StateEstimator:\n    \"\"\"Particle filter + online slip & heading bias & solar efficiency estimator.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, surf: Surface, bx: float, by: float):\n        self.surf = surf\n        self.bx, self.by = bx, by\n        self.rng = random.Random(1001)\n        self.slip_base_est = {\n            \"bedrock\": 0.03,\n            \"regolith\": 0.085,\n            \"sand\": 0.25,\n self.n_particles]\n            for i in range(self.n_particles)\n        ]\n        self.x_est = bx\n        self.y_est = by\n        self.bias_est = 0.0\n\n    def process_packet(self, pkt: Dict[str, Any], scheduled_cmds: Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]]) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Process one newly arrived telemetry packet. Returns True if a new target was discovered.\"\"\"\n        t = pkt[\"tick\"]\n        if t <= self.last_obs_tick:\n            return False\n\n        new_discovery = False\n        for ev in self.discovered_targets:\n                    self.discovered_targets[tgt[\"id\"]] = tgt\n                    new_discovery = True\n            elif isinstance(ev, dict) and ev.get(\"type\") ==          pb = max(-10.0, min(10.0, pb))\n                h_true = cmd[\"heading_deg\"] + pb + self.rng.gauss(0.0, 1.5)\n                s_noise = self.rng.uniform(-0.025, 0.025)\n                out =          # Compute log-likelihood\n            ll = -0.5 * ((px - obs_x) ** 2 + (py - obs_y) ** 2) / (1.0 ** 2)\n            if obs_wo > 0.0:\n                sig_vo = max(0.05, 0.05 * g_dist)\n \"\"\"Forward-simulate from last_obs_tick to target_tick through scheduled commands.\"\"\"\n        px, py = self.x_est, self.y_est\n        bat = self.last_battery\n        for tk in \"\"\"Returns (penalized_ticks, true_ticks) or None if unsafe.\"\"\"\n        dist = math.hypot(x1 - x0, y1 - y0)\n        if dist < 1e-6:\n            return 0.0, 0.0\n        ux, uy = (x1 - x0) / dist, (y1 - y0) / dist\n        n_sub = max(2, int(math.ceil(dist / 2.0)))\n        sub_d = dist / n_sub\n        gain = PHYSICS[\"slip_grade_gain\"]\n        pen_cost = 0.0\n        true_cost = 0.0\n        for s in range(n_sub + 1):\n            frac = s / n_sub\n            sx = x0 + (x1 - x0) * frac\n            sy = y0 + (y1 - y0) * frac\n            if self.get_clearance(sx, sy) < float) -> Tuple[int, int]:\n        ci = min(self.nx - 1, max(0, int(round(x / self.step))))\n        cj = min(self.ny - 1, max(0, int(round(y / self.step))))\n        if self.adj[cj][ci]:\n dijkstra(self, si: int, sj: int):\n        key = (si, sj)\n        if key in self._dijkstra_cache:\n            return self._dijkstra_cache[key]\n        dist_p = [[1e9] * self.nx for _ in range(self.ny)]\n        dist_t = [[1e9] * self.nx for _ in range(self.ny)]\n        parent: List[List[Optional[Tuple[int, int]]]] = [[None] * self.nx for _ in range(self.ny)]\n        dist_p[sj][si]     direct = self.edge_costs(x0, y0, x1, y1, self.slip_base, min_clear=7.5)\n        si, sj = self.nearest_valid_node(x0, y0)\n        ti, tj = self.nearest_valid_node(x1, y1)\n        dist_p, _, StateEstimator:\n    \"\"\"Particle filter + online slip & heading bias & solar efficiency estimator.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, surf: Surface, bx: float, by: float):\n        self.surf = surf\n        self.bx, self.by = bx, by\n        self.rng = random.Random(1001)\n        self.slip_base_est = {\n            \"bedrock\": 0.03,\n            \"regolith\": 0.085,\n            \"sand\": 0.25,\n ]\n        self.x_est = bx\n        self.y_est = by\n        self.bias_est = 0.0\n\n    def process_packet(self, pkt: Dict[str, Any], scheduled_cmds: Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]]) -> bool:\n        t = pkt[\"tick\"]\n        if t <= self.last_obs_tick:\n            return False\n\n        new_discovery = False\n        for ev in pkt.get(\"events\", []):\n            if isinstance(ev, dict) and self.last_battery + charge < 99.8:\n            eff = charge / sun_factor\n            if 0.78 <= eff <= 1.02:\n                self.solar_eff_est = max(0.8, min(1.0, eff))\n\n        cmd = self.slip_obs_den[obs_ter] += 1.0\n                self.slip_base_est[obs_ter] = self.slip_obs_num[obs_ter] / self.slip_obs_den[obs_ter]\n\n        log_weights = []\n        valid_energy_obs = obs_wo > 0.0:\n                sig_vo = max(0.05, 0.05 * g_dist)\n                ll += -0.5 * ((obs_vo - g_dist) / sig_vo) ** 2\n            sl = self.surf.slope(px, py)\n            ll += -0.5 * self.particles)\n\n        ess = 1.0 / sum(p[3] ** 2 for p in self.particles)\n        if ess < 0.5 * self.n_particles:\n            new_p = []\n            step = 1.0 / self.n_particles\n            r0 = self.rng.uniform(0.0, step)\n            c = self.particles[0][3]\n            idx = 0\n            for m in range(self.n_particles):\n                u = r0 + m * step\n                while u > c and idx < self.n_particles - 1:\n                    idx += 1\n                    c += self.particles[idx][3]\n                bx_p, by_p, bb_p, _ = self.particles[idx]\n                nx_p = \"\"\"Targets either confirmed sampled in telemetry or scheduled to be sampled before up_to_tick.\"\"\"\n        s = set(self.estimator.sampled_targets)\n        for tk, tid in    done = self.active_sampled_set(start_tick)\n        cur = [tid for tid in self.tour if tid not in done and tid in self.targets_by_id]\n        # Drop tail targets if current tour exceeds max_return_tick=398\n        while cur and self.eval_sequence(cur, start_x, start_y, start_tick, 398)[0] < 0:\n            # Find which single target removal gives best valid or shortest tour\n            best_rem = None\n            best_sc = -1e18\n            best_et = 1e18\n            for i in range(len(cur)):\n                cand = cur[:i] + cur[i+1:]\n                sc, et =      cur = best_rem if best_rem is not None else cur[:-1]\n\n        cur_sc, cur_et = self.eval_sequence(cur, start_x, start_y, start_tick, 398)\n        all_avail = [tid for tid in self.targets_by_id Estimate initial metres\n        _, sgx, sgy, code = self.surf.sample(x0, y0)\n        tname = TERRAIN_CODES[code]\n        rad = math.radians(desired_deg)\n        grade = sgx * math.cos(rad) + sgy * math.sin(rad)\n        slip0 = self.estimator.slip_base_est[tname] + PHYSICS[\"slip_grade_gain\"][tname] * max(0.0, grade)\n        slip0 = min(0.85, max(0.0, slip0))\n        cmd_m = min(5.0, max(0.1, dist / (1.0 - slip0))) if exact_stop else 5.0\n\n        # 3 iterations of shooting to compensate side-slip and forward slip\n        for _ in range(3):\n            out = drive(self.surf, PHYSICS, x0, y0, cmd_h + self.estimator.bias_est, cmd_m,\n                        self.estimator.slip_base_est, noise=0.0)\n            ach_dx, ach_dy = out[\"x\"] - x0, out[\"y\"] - y0\n            ach_dist = math.hypot(ach_dx, ach_dy)\n            if ach_dist > 1e-4:\n                ach_deg = math.degrees(math.atan2(ach_dy, ach_dx))\n                err_deg = wrap_deg(desired_deg - ach_deg)\n                cmd_h = wrap_deg(cmd_h + err_deg)\n                if exact_stop:\n                    cmd_m = min(5.0, max(0.1, cmd_m * (dist / ach_dist)))\n\n        # Emergency return to base check\n        ticks_to_base = self.planner.true_travel_ticks(px, py, self.bx, self.by)\n        must_return = (exec_tick + math.ceil(ticks_to_base * 1.08) + 8 >= self.rules[\"max_ticks\"])\n\n        if must_return or not self.tour:\n            # Check if we are already at base\n            d_base = math.hypot(px - self.bx, py - self.by)\n            if d_base <= 1.5:\n                cmd = {\"action\": \"wait\"}\n            else:\n                path = self.planner.find_path(px, py, self.bx, self.by)\n                wp_x, wp_y = path[1] if len(path) > 1 else (self.bx, self.by)\n                exact = (len(path) <= 2 and d_base <= 4.8)\n                cmd = self.solve_drive_command(px, py, wp_x, wp_y, exact_stop=exact)\n        else:\n            tid = self.tour[0]\n            tgt = self.targets_by_id[tid]\n            tx, ty = tgt[\"x\"], tgt[\"y\"]\n            d_tgt = math.hypot(px - tx, py - ty)\n\n            if d_tgt <= 1.35:\n                # Safely inside target_radius (2.5m) with >1.1m margin! Sample now!\n                cmd = {\"action\": \"sample\"}\n                self.scheduled_sample_tick[exec_tick] = tid\n            else:\n                # Aim slightly shifted (0.6m) toward next target/base if on final step so we cut the corner safely\n                aim_x, aim_y = tx, ty\n                if d_tgt <= 5.5:\n                    nxt_x, nxt_y =     nd = math.hypot(ndx, ndy)\n                    if nd > 1.0:\n                        aim_x = tx + 0.55 * (ndx / nd)\n                        aim_y = ty + 0.55 * (ndy / nd)\n\n                path self.solve_drive_command(px, py, wp_x, wp_y, exact_stop=True)\n                else:\n                    # If path[1] is closer than 4.5m and there is a path[2], interpolate along path so we do a full 5m drive\n                    cmd = self.solve_drive_command(px, py, wp_x, wp_y, exact_stop=False)\n\n        # Battery floor guard: if predicted battery after command would drop below 12.0, wait dist_e = [[1e9] * self.nx for _ in range(self.ny)]\n        parent: List[List[Optional[Tuple[int, int]]]] = [[None] * self.nx for _ in range(self.ny)]\n        dist_p[sj][si] = 0.0\n        dist_t[sj][si] = 0.0\n        dist_e[sj][si] = 0.0\n        pq = [(0.0, si, sj)]\n        while pq:\n            d, i, j = heapq.heappop(pq)\n            if d > dist_p[j][i] + 1e-6:\n                continue\n            t_cur = dist_t[j][i]\n            e_cur = dist_e[j][i]\n            for ni, nj, pc, tc, ec in self.adj[j][i]:\n                nd = d + pc\n                if nd < dist_p[nj][ni]:\n                    dist_p[nj][ni] = nd\n                    dist_t[nj][ni] = t_cur + tc\n                    dist_e[nj][ni] = e_cur + ec\n                    parent[nj][ni] = (i, j)\n                    heapq.heappush(pq, (nd, ni, nj))\n        self._dijkstra_cache[key] = (dist_p, dist_t, dist_e, parent)\n        return dist_p, dist_t, dist_e, parent\n\n    def true_travel_ticks_and_energy(self, x0: float, y0: float, x1: float, y1: float) -> Tuple[float, float]:\n        si, sj = self.nearest_valid_node(x0, y0)\n        ti, tj = self.nearest_valid_node(x1, y1)\n        _, dist_t, dist_e, _ = self.dijkstra(si, sj)\n        d_start = math.hypot(x0 - si * self.step, y0 - sj * self.step) / 4.5\n        return max(0.0, dist_t[tj][ti] + d_start * 0.3), max(0.0, dist_e[tj][ti] + d_start * 0.6)\n\n    def true_travel_ticks(self, x0: float, y0: float, x1: float, y1: float) -> float:\n        return self.true_travel_ticks_and_energy(x0, y0, x1, y1)[0]\n\n    def find_path(self, x0: float, y0: float, x1: float, y1: float) -> List[Tuple[float, float]]:\n        direct = self.edge_costs(x0, y0, x1, y1, self.slip_base, min_clear=7.5)\n        si, sj = self.nearest_valid_node(x0, y0)\n        ti, tj = self.nearest_valid_node(x1, y1)\n        dist_p, _, _, parent = self.dijkstra(si, sj)\n        grid_p = dist_p[tj][ti]\n        if direct is not None and direct[0] <= grid_p * 1.03 + 0.3:\n            return [(x0, y0), (x1, y1)]\n\n        pts = [(x1, y1)]\n        cur = (ti, tj)\n        while cur is not None:\n            ci, cj = cur\n            if math.hypot(ci * self.step - pts[-1][0], cj * self.step - pts[-1][1]) > 0.5:\n                pts.append((ci * self.step, cj * self.step))\n            if cur == (si, sj):\n                break\n            cur = parent[cj][ci]\n        if math.hypot(x0 - pts[-1][0], y0 - pts[-1][1]) > 0.5:\n            pts.append((x0, y0))\n        pts.reverse()\n\n        cum_p = [0.0] * len(pts)\n        for k in range(len(pts) - 1):\n            ec = self.edge_costs(pts[k][0], pts[k][1], pts[k+1][0], pts[k+1][1], self.slip_base, min_clear=5.0)\n            cum_p[k+1] = cum_p[k] + (ec[0] if ec is not None else 1.0)\n\n        smoothed = [pts[0]]\n        idx = 0\n        while idx < len(pts) - 1:\n            best_next = idx + 1\n            for nxt in range(len(pts) - 1, idx + 1, -1):\n                if math.hypot(pts[nxt][0] - pts[idx][0], pts[nxt][1] - pts[idx][1]) > 40.0:\n                    continue\n                dc = self.edge_costs(pts[idx][0], pts[idx][1], pts[nxt][0], pts[nxt][1], self.slip_base, min_clear=7.5)\n                if dc is not None and dc[0] <= (cum_p[nxt] - cum_p[idx]) * 1.02 + 0.08:\n                    best_next = nxt\n                    break\n            smoothed.append(pts[best_next])\n            idx = best_next\n        return smoothed\n\n\nclass StateEstimator:\n    \"\"\"Particle filter + online slip & heading bias & solar efficiency estimator.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, surf: Surface, bx: float, by: float):\n        self.surf = surf\n        self.bx, self.by = bx, by\n        self.rng = random.Random(1001)\n        self.slip_base_est = {\n            \"bedrock\": 0.03,\n            \"regolith\": 0.085,\n            \"sand\": 0.25,\n            \"rocks\": 0.18,\n        }\n        self.slip_obs_num = {k: 2.0 * v for k, v in self.slip_base_est.items()}\n        self.slip_obs_den = {k: 2.0 for k in self.slip_base_est}\n        self.solar_eff_est = 0.90\n        self.solar_eff_known = False\n        self.last_obs_tick = 0\n        self.last_battery = 100.0\n        self.sampled_targets = set()\n        self.discovered_targets: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n\n        self.n_particles = 300\n        self.particles = [\n            [bx, by, -5.0 + 10.0 * i / (self.n_particles - 1), 1.0 / self.n_particles]\n            for i in range(self.n_particles)\n        ]\n        self.x_est = bx\n        self.y_est = by\n        self.bias_est = 0.0\n\n    def process_packet(self, pkt: Dict[str, Any], scheduled_cmds: Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]]) -> bool:\n        t = pkt[\"tick\"]\n        if t <= self.last_obs_tick:\n            return False\n\n        new_discovery = False\n        for ev in pkt.get(\"events\", []):\n            if isinstance(ev, dict) and ev.get(\"type\") == \"target_discovered\":\n                tgt = ev.get(\"target\")\n                if tgt and tgt[\"id\"] not in self.discovered_targets:\n                    self.discovered_targets[tgt[\"id\"]] = tgt\n                    new_discovery = True\n            elif isinstance(ev, dict) and ev.get(\"type\") == \"safety_stop\":\n                for k in list(scheduled_cmds.keys()):\n                    if k > t:\n                        scheduled_cmds.pop(k, None)\n\n        for tid in pkt.get(\"sampled_targets\", []):\n            self.sampled_targets.add(tid)\n\n        charge = pkt[\"charge\"]\n        bat = pkt[\"battery\"]\n        sun_factor = 2.8 * math.sin(math.pi * (t + 56.0) / 532.0)\n        if sun_factor > 0.1 and self.last_battery + charge < 99.8:\n            eff = charge / sun_factor\n            if 0.78 <= eff <= 1.02:\n                self.solar_eff_est = max(0.8, min(1.0, eff))\n                self.solar_eff_known = True\n\n        cmd = scheduled_cmds.get(t)\n        obs_x, obs_y = pkt[\"position\"]\n        obs_vo = pkt[\"visual_odometry\"]\n        obs_wo = pkt[\"wheel_odometry\"]\n        obs_ter = pkt[\"terrain\"]\n        obs_tilt = pkt[\"tilt\"]\n\n        if cmd and cmd[\"action\"] == \"drive\" and obs_wo >= 1.5 and abs(obs_wo - cmd[\"metres\"]) < 0.05:\n            ter0 = self.surf.terrain(self.x_est, self.y_est)\n            if ter0 == obs_ter:\n                lo, hi = PHYSICS[\"slip_base_ranges\"][obs_ter]\n                best_s = self.slip_base_est[obs_ter]\n                best_err = 1e9\n                for idx_s in range(13):\n                    s_cand = lo + (hi - lo) * idx_s / 12.0\n                    sb = dict(self.slip_base_est)\n                    sb[obs_ter] = s_cand\n                    out = drive(self.surf, PHYSICS, self.x_est, self.y_est,\n                                cmd[\"heading_deg\"] + self.bias_est, obs_wo, sb, noise=0.0)\n                    err = abs(out[\"ground\"] - obs_vo)\n                    if err < best_err:\n                        best_err = err\n                        best_s = s_cand\n                self.slip_obs_num[obs_ter] += best_s\n                self.slip_obs_den[obs_ter] += 1.0\n                self.slip_base_est[obs_ter] = self.slip_obs_num[obs_ter] / self.slip_obs_den[obs_ter]\n\n        log_weights = []\n        valid_energy_obs = (self.last_battery + charge < 99.8) and (bat > 0.2)\n        obs_spent = self.last_battery + charge - bat - 0.15\n\n        for p in self.particles:\n            px, py, pb, pw = p\n            if cmd and cmd[\"action\"] == \"drive\" and obs_wo > 0.0:\n                pb += self.rng.gauss(0.0, 0.25)\n                pb = max(-10.0, min(10.0, pb))\n                h_true = cmd[\"heading_deg\"] + pb + self.rng.gauss(0.0, 1.5)\n                s_noise = self.rng.uniform(-0.025, 0.025)\n                out = drive(self.surf, PHYSICS, px, py, h_true, obs_wo, self.slip_base_est, noise=s_noise)\n                px, py = out[\"x\"], out[\"y\"]\n                g_dist = out[\"ground\"]\n                e_spent = out[\"energy\"]\n            else:\n                g_dist = 0.0\n                e_spent = 2.0 if (cmd and cmd[\"action\"] == \"sample\") else 0.0\n\n            p[0], p[1], p[2] = px, py, pb\n\n            ll = -0.5 * ((px - obs_x) ** 2 + (py - obs_y) ** 2) / (1.0 ** 2)\n            if obs_wo > 0.0:\n                sig_vo = max(0.05, 0.05 * g_dist)\n                ll += -0.5 * ((obs_vo - g_dist) / sig_vo) ** 2\n            sl = self.surf.slope(px, py)\n            ll += -0.5 * ((sl - obs_tilt) / 0.015) ** 2\n            if self.surf.terrain(px, py) != obs_ter:\n                ll -= 2.0\n            if valid_energy_obs and obs_wo > 0.0:\n                ll += -0.5 * ((e_spent - obs_spent) / 0.08) ** 2\n            log_weights.append(math.log(max(1e-12, pw)) + ll)\n\n        max_lw = max(log_weights)\n        tot_w = 0.0\n        for i, lw in enumerate(log_weights):\n            w = math.exp(max(-15.0, lw - max_lw))\n            self.particles[i][3] = w\n            tot_w += w\n\n        for p in self.particles:\n            p[3] /= tot_w\n\n        self.x_est = sum(p[0] * p[3] for p in self.particles)\n        self.y_est = sum(p[1] * p[3] for p in self.particles)\n        self.bias_est = sum(p[2] * p[3] for p in self.particles)\n\n        ess = 1.0 / sum(p[3] ** 2 for p in self.particles)\n        if ess < 0.5 * self.n_particles:\n            new_p = []\n            step = 1.0 / self.n_particles\n            r0 = self.rng.uniform(0.0, step)\n            c = self.particles[0][3]\n            idx = 0\n            for m in range(self.n_particles):\n                u = r0 + m * step\n                while u > c and idx < self.n_particles - 1:\n                    idx += 1\n                    c += self.particles[idx][3]\n                bx_p, by_p, bb_p, _ = self.particles[idx]\n                nx_p = min(self.surf.width, max(0.0, bx_p + self.rng.gauss(0.0, 0.08)))\n                ny_p = min(self.surf.height, max(0.0, by_p + self.rng.gauss(0.0, 0.08)))\n                nb_p = max(-10.0, min(10.0, bb_p + self.rng.gauss(0.0, 0.12)))\n                new_p.append([nx_p, ny_p, nb_p, step])\n            self.particles = new_p\n\n        self.last_obs_tick = t\n        self.last_battery = bat\n        return new_discovery\n\n    def predict_state_at(self, target_tick: int, scheduled_cmds: Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]]) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:\n        px, py = self.x_est, self.y_est\n        bat = self.last_battery\n        for tk in range(self.last_obs_tick + 1, target_tick + 1):\n            charge = 2.8 * self.solar_eff_est * math.sin(math.pi * (tk + 56.0) / 532.0)\n            bat = min(100.0, bat + charge) - 0.15\n            cmd = scheduled_cmds.get(tk)\n            if cmd is not None:\n                if cmd[\"action\"] == \"drive\":\n                    out = drive(self.surf, PHYSICS, px, py,\n                                cmd[\"heading_deg\"] + self.bias_est, cmd[\"metres\"],\n                                self.slip_base_est, noise=0.0)\n                    px, py = out[\"x\"], out[\"y\"]\n                    bat -= out[\"energy\"]\n                elif cmd[\"action\"] == \"sample\":\n                    bat -= 2.0\n        return px, py, bat\n\n\ndef wrap_deg(angle: float) -> float:\n    return (angle + 180.0) % 360.0 - 180.0\n\n\nclass MissionController:\n    \"\"\"Complete closed-loop predictive mission controller.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, rules_data: Dict[str, Any], map_data: self.scheduled_sample_tick.items():\n            if self.estimator.last_obs_tick < tk <= up_to_tick:\n                s.add(tid)\n        return s\n\n    def eval_sequence(self, seq: List[str], start_x: float, start_y: float, start_tick: int, start_bat: float = 95.0, max_return_tick: int = 398) -> Tuple[float, float]:\n        t = float(start_tick)\n        bat = float(start_bat)\n        cx, cy = start_x, start_y\n        score = 0.0\n        eff = self.estimator.solar_eff_est\n        for tid in seq:\n            tgt = self.targets_by_id[tid]\n            tx, ty = tgt[\"x\"], tgt[\"y\"]\n            raw_t, raw_e = self.planner.true_travel_ticks_and_energy(cx, cy, tx, ty)\n            drive_t = max(1.0, raw_t * 1.04 + 0.35)\n            leg_t = drive_t + 1.0\n            t_mid = min(420.0, t + 0.5 * leg_t)\n            solar_rate = 2.8 * eff * math.sin(math.pi * (t_mid + 56.0) / 532.0)\n            net_charge = leg_t * (solar_rate - 0.15)\n            leg_e = raw_e * 1.05 + 2.0\n            bat = min(100.0, bat + net_charge) - leg_e\n            if bat < 10.0:\n                deficit = 10.0 - bat\n                recharge_rate = max(0.35, solar_rate - 0.15)\n                wait_t = deficit / recharge_rate\n                t += wait_t\n                bat = 10.0\n            t += leg_t\n            st = int(math.ceil(t))\n            dl = tgt[\"deadline_tick\"]\n            val = tgt[\"value\"] if (dl is None or st <= dl) else tgt[\"late_value\"]\n            score += val\n            cx, cy = tx, ty\n\n        ret_raw_t, ret_raw_e = self.planner.true_travel_ticks_and_energy(cx, cy, self.bx, self.by)\n        ret_t = ret_raw_t * 1.05 + 0.5\n        t_mid = min(420.0, t + 0.5 * ret_t)\n        solar_rate = 2.8 * eff * math.sin(math.pi * (t_mid + 56.0) / 532.0)\n        bat = min(100.0, bat + ret_t * (solar_rate - 0.15)) - ret_raw_e * 1.05\n        if bat < 9.0:\n            deficit = 9.0 - bat\n            recharge_rate = max(0.35, solar_rate - 0.15)\n            ret_t += deficit / recharge_rate\n        end_t = t + ret_t\n        if end_t > max_return_tick:\n            return -1e9, end_t\n end_t without the max_return_tick cliff, used for trimming over-long tours.\"\"\"\n        sc, et = self.eval_sequence(seq, start_x, start_y, start_tick, start_bat, max_return_tick=9999)\n        return sc, et\n\n    def reoptimize_tour(self, start_x: float, start_y: float, start_tick: int, start_bat: float) -> None:\n        done = self.active_sampled_set(start_tick)\n        cur = [tid for tid in self.tour if tid not in done and tid in self.targets_by_id]\n\n        # Trim lowest value-loss / time-saved target if over max_return_tick\n        while cur and self.eval_sequence(cur, start_x, start_y, start_tick, start_bat, 398)[0] < 0:\n            full_sc, full_et = self.raw_sequence_score_and_time(cur, start_x, start_y, start_tick, start_bat)\n            best_rem = None\n            best_valid_sc = -1e18\n            best_ratio = 1e18\n            for i in range(len(cur)):\n                cand = cur[:i] + cur[i+1:]\n                v_sc, v_et = self.eval_sequence(cand, start_x, start_y, start_tick, start_bat, 398)\n                if v_sc >= 0:\n                    if v_sc > best_valid_sc:\n                        best_valid_sc = v_sc\n                        best_rem = cand\n                elif best_valid_sc < 0:\n                    r_sc, r_et = self.raw_sequence_score_and_time(cand, start_x, start_y, start_tick, start_bat)\n                    time_saved = max(0.5, full_et - r_et)\n                    val_lost = max(0.0, full_sc - r_sc)\n                    ratio = val_lost / time_saved\n                    if ratio < best_ratio:\n                        best_ratio = ratio\n                        best_rem = cand\n            cur = best_rem if best_rem is not None else cur[:-1]\n\n        cur_sc, cur_et = self.eval_sequence(cur, start_x, start_y, start_tick, start_bat, 398)\n        all_avail = [tid for tid in self.targets_by_id if tid not in done]\n\n        improved = True\n        iters = 0\n        while improved and iters < 30:\n            iters += 1\n            improved = False\n            in_set = set(cur)\n            # 1. Insert unvisited target\n            for x in all_avail:\n                if x in in_set:\n                    continue\n                for j in range(len(cur) + 1):\n                    cand = cur[:j] + [x] + cur[j:]\n                    sc, et = self.eval_sequence(cand, start_x, start_y, start_tick, start_bat, 398)\n                    if sc > cur_sc + 1e-6 or (abs(sc - cur_sc) <= 1e-6 and et < cur_et - 0.1):\n                        cur, cur_sc, cur_et = cand, sc, et\n                        improved = True\n                        break\n                if improved:\n                    break\n            if improved:\n                continue\n\n            # 2. Replace 1 visited target with 1 unvisited target\n            for i in range(len(cur)):\n                rem = cur[:i] + cur[i+1:]\n                for x in all_avail:\n                    if x in in_set:\n                        continue\n                    for j in range(len(rem) + 1):\n                        cand = rem[:j] + [x] + rem[j:]\n                        sc, et = self.eval_sequence(cand, start_x, start_y, start_tick, start_bat, 398)\n                        if sc > cur_sc + 1e-6 or (abs(sc - cur_sc) <= 1e-6 and et < cur_et - 0.1):\n                            cur, cur_sc, cur_et = cand, sc, et\n                            improved = True\n                            break\n                    if improved:\n                        break\n                if improved:\n                    break\n            if improved:\n                continue\n\n            # 3. Relocate within tour\n            for i in range(len(cur)):\n                x = cur[i]\n                rem = cur[:i] + cur[i+1:]\n                for j in range(len(rem) + 1):\n                    if j == i:\n                        continue\n                    cand = rem[:j] + [x] + rem[j:]\n                    sc, et = self.eval_sequence(cand, start_x, start_y, start_tick, start_bat, 398)\n                   break\n                if improved:\n                    break\n\n        self.tour = cur\n\n    def solve_drive_command(self, x0: float, y0: float, gx: float, gy: float, exact_stop: bool) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        dx, dy = gx - x0, gy - y0\n        dist = math.hypot(dx, dy)\n        if dist < 0.05:\n            return {\"action\": \"wait\"}\n\n        desired_deg = math.degrees(math.atan2(dy, dx))\n        cmd_h = wrap_deg(desired_deg - self.estimator.bias_est)\n\n        _, sgx, sgy, code = self.surf.sample(x0, y0)\n        tname = TERRAIN_CODES[code]\n        rad = math.radians(desired_deg)\n        grade = sgx * math.cos(rad) + sgy * math.sin(rad)\n        slip0 = self.estimator.slip_base_est[tname] + PHYSICS[\"slip_grade_gain\"][tname] * max(0.0, grade)\n        slip0 = min(0.85, max(0.0, slip0))\n        cmd_m = min(5.0, max(0.1, dist / (1.0 - slip0))) if exact_stop else 5.0\n\n        for _ in range(3):\n            out = drive(self.surf, PHYSICS, x0, y0, cmd_h + self.estimator.bias_est, cmd_m,\n                        self.estimator.slip_base_est, noise=0.0)\n            ach_dx, ach_dy = out[\"x\"] - x0, out[\"y\"] - y0\n            ach_dist = math.hypot(ach_dx, ach_dy)\n            if ach_dist > 1e-4:\n                ach_deg = math.degrees(math.atan2(ach_dy, ach_dx))\n                err_deg = wrap_deg(desired_deg - ach_deg)\n                cmd_h = wrap_deg(cmd_h + err_deg)\n                if exact_stop:\n                    cmd_m = min(5.0, max(0.1, cmd_m * (dist / ach_dist)))\n\n        for dh in (0.0, -4.5, 4.5, -8.0, 8.0):\n            out_chk = drive(self.surf, PHYSICS, x0, y0, cmd_h + self.estimator.bias_est + dh, cmd_m,\n                            self.estimator.slip_base_est, noise=-0.02)\n            if for cand_offset in range(-60, 65, 5):\n                    ch = wrap_deg(cmd_h + cand_offset)\n                    o2 = drive(self.surf, PHYSICS, x0, y0, ch + self.estimator.bias_est, cmd_m,\n
